# BUDLUM LEAF · THE DIRECTIVE · FINAL EDITION v5.0 · REVISION L.7 candidate · 2026-10-19 · the assembled single source of truth

*This file is the concatenation of the twenty-four parts of `LEAF/FINAL/`, built by `LEAF/tools/assemble.py`. Edit the parts, never this file. The counts of the gates and of the gaps live in `LEAF/tools/canon.json` and are checked by gate PRG-15.*

# BUDLUM LEAF · THE DIRECTIVE · FINAL EDITION v5.0

**Date:** 2026-10-20 · **Owner:** the Leaf Program Lead · **Status:** the single source of truth for the program

**Battery-free authority, Revision L.8**. Part 24 governs all earlier parts on panel energy, stack, module placement, charging, security and attachment.
The panel has no battery and is passive without its attached Core. `2.00 mm` is an upper limit, not a thickness target.
Earlier panel cell, Qi receiver and distant-Core product designs are retired. Historic data stays in the audit record, not in a live specification. `[DECISION]`

**Supersedes by consolidation:** `BUDLUM-LEAF-DIRECTIVE.md` v3.1 and the working annex set `MASTER/00` to `MASTER/16`. Those files remain as the detailed record; **this document is what is built from**.

---

## PART 0 · HOW TO READ THIS DIRECTIVE

### 0.1 The promise of this document

This directive takes Budlum Leaf from a sentence to a buildable system. It covers the product law, the geometry, the panel, the body, the energy, the radio and the camera module. It covers the glasses, the field mode, the firmware and the network. It covers the security model, the verification plan and the program that builds it. The **how** is written at the depth of a work order, and not of a brochure. A person or an AI can start work on any part of it. This document and the Budlum repository answer the question already. `[DECISION]`

### 0.2 The tag system

| Tag | The meaning |
|---|---|
| `[DECISION]` | fixed by Ayaz or by this directive; only a new answer of Ayaz changes it |
| `[PUBLICATION]` | a measured or a peer reviewed source |
| `[COMPANY]` | a supplier or a product statement |
| `[TREE]` | an artefact read from the Budlum repository |
| `[HYPOTHESIS]` | a design target with a number, to be replaced by a measurement at a named gate |
| `[GENERAL]` | physics or a general engineering rule |

**A number without a tag is a defect. A public claim without a gate is a defect. `[DECISION]`**

### 0.3 The ten laws

| No. | The law | Where it is enforced |
|---|---|---|
| L1 | The product is one system that changes its surface, not five products | Part 1, Part 8 |
| L2 | The body holds no processor, no operating system, no camera, no speaker, no jack and no cooling part | Part 3, Part 6 |
| L3 | Every gram that is not display, energy, radio or anchor is a design error | Part 3 |
| L4 | The user sees the world with the user's own eyes | Part 7 |
| L5 | The interaction is answered inside the device; the heavy work is answered by the verified network | Part 10 |
| L6 | No read without a grant, no sensor without a lease, no model without a declaration | Part 11 |
| L7 | A claim needs a gate, a raw data set and a date | Part 12 |
| L8 | The product is a Budlum Leaf; a device class word is never a product name | Part 1 |
| L9 | A part is renewable, and a part carries a record | Part 4, Part 6 |
| L10 | The program does not close; each block leaves the workspace further ahead | Part 13 |

### 0.4 The one-paragraph system

A Budlum Leaf is a light, flexible and emissive surface. It carries a removable energy sheet, a wallet authority and a radio. The interface is rendered inside the device. The correctness, the storage and the heavy compute run on the Budlum network. The session, the data and the identity move between providers, and the device does not change. `[DECISION]`

### 0.7 What is fixed and what is open

**Fixed:** the geometry, the mass law, the panel, the light technology and the module. **Fixed:** the naming, the design language, the family, the market and the production country. **Fixed:** the network position. **The thickness is an envelope: 4.00 mm is the maximum, and the order states the body thickness inside it** (the audit F-09).
**Open, and not blocking**. The catalogue band is parked by the order of Ayaz. The launch plan is parked. The cost items are deferred. The second source names are a partner question. The three sensor suppliers and the exact cell partner are open.

**The rule**. An open item never blocks a work package with a written order and a gate. It blocks a claim. `[DECISION]`

| "176 ppd at the eye" for the Frame came from the flat screen formula and is wrong | the near eye rule: **pixels across divided by the field across**. The Frame uses a 4K class microdisplay at about 2 400 ppi. The Field mode is about 20 to 25 ppd | Part 15, H-1 |
| two thickness values for one face (0.46 and 0.59) | the ledger is single: 0.46 + 2.88 + 0.46 + 0.06 + 0.04 = **3.90 mm nominal, 4.00 mm maximum** | Part 15, H-2 |
| the gate count said 90 in one part and 89 in another | **336 gates: 300 device and network, 16 program and 20 research** at the L.7 candidate, and the count comes from `tools/canon.json` by gate PRG-15 | Part 15, H-3 and H-16 |

**The rule introduced by the pass**. An audit of the numbers and of the physics runs at every revision. Its findings are published in this part, and not quietly fixed. `[DECISION]`

### 0.76 The external audit pass of 2026-10-05, and the four tools

**The audit**. An external audit of revision K arrived with it: `AUDIT/LEAF-AUDIT-AND-HARDENING.md`, fifteen findings, each with a severity and a work order. **Every finding is closed by an edited file and a changed gate**, and the ledger is Part 19, Section 19.5. **Eight corrections matter most**. The energy density became a band. The strain rule became `d / R`, measured about each layer's own axis. The layers decouple and every interface shears. Polycarbonate keeps the whole face, because its worst strain is 0.10 percent. The book model retired and the wrap at R 25.5 mm replaced it, with the gates B-1 to B-10. The water class became one claim. The thickness became an envelope. The second face, the custom shape and the patent screen gained their rules.

**The four tools, and the gates they carry**.

| The tool | The gate | The duty |
|---|---|---|
| `tools/assemble.py` | **PRG-16** | build the single source of truth from the parts: each part once, in ascending order |
| `tools/check_prose.py` | **PRG-12** | no em dash, 25 words for a descriptive sentence and 20 for a procedural one |
| `tools/check_crossrefs.py` with `tools/canon.json` | **PRG-15** | one number per count, held in one file, measured against every claim |
| `pafta/check_sheets.py` | the drawing line | the overflow, the collision and the covered text of every sheet |

**The measurement of Revision L.6.0**. `FINAL/`, `HARDWARE/` and `MASTER/` pass at `0` em dashes, `0` long sentences, `0` long cells and `0` warning words. The three `AUDIT/` arena reports and `NEREDE-NE-VAR.md` still carry findings from before L.6.0. Those findings are recorded, and they are not hidden. 336 gates of 300 device and network, 16 program and 20 research. 54 gap items of 17 blockers, 30 major and 7 minor. `[DECISION]`

### 0.77 The block of 2026-10-12: 4-Quadrant (`+`) 2.00 mm (`68 g`) solid-state body, universal `Qi2.2`, and `90°`-bent-stem B module (`20 g`)

**Five orders of Ayaz opened Revision L.5.0**. *Cancel the bulky external bottom battery module*. *Split the `2.00 mm` (`68 g`) body across `+` into four `17 g` quadrants with a `<15 µm` (`n = 1.585`) seam*. *Use an eyewear-safe biocompatible `LLZO/PEO` all-solid-state battery (`4 x 3.0 Wh = 12.0 Wh`, `<=36.2 C`, `ISO 10993`)*. *Enable `Qi / Qi2.2` wireless charging on the portless screen body and put `USB Type-C` only in the B module*. *Bend only the B stem (`çubuk`) at `90°` (`dik açıyla`) while its round ring lies flat on the screen (`+Z` or `-Z`)*.

**What landed**.

| The change | Where |
|---|---|
| **portless universal `Qi2.2` wireless charging on the screen body**. Central `Ø54 mm` `Qi2.2` (`15-25 W`) and `4 x Ø22 mm` `Qi` coils charge on any pad; zero USB-C on body | Part 20, Section 20.1 and Section 20.2; `HARDWARE/04`, `HARDWARE/14`, `pafta/P10`, `pafta/P16` |
| **only the stem (`çubuk`) of the B module (`20.0 g`) bends `90°` (`dik açıyla`) while the `Ø32/Ø24 mm` ring lies flat on the screen**. Clips onto all 4 edges and flips between Front (`+Z`) and Back (`-Z`) | Part 20, Sections 20.3.1 to 20.3.5; `HARDWARE/05`, `HARDWARE/13`, `HARDWARE/20`, `pafta/P14`, `pafta/P21` |
| **USB Type-C (`40 W PD 3.1 + DP 2.1`) lives exclusively in the `90°`-bent B stem**. All non-wireless functions (`U1` TCON, `U2` `UWB / mmWave` SiP, camera, audio, RF, wired USB-C) require the B module | Part 20, Section 20.3.6; `HARDWARE/04-PORT-RAIL.md`, `HARDWARE/11-THE-ENERGY-SHEET.md` |
| **4-Quadrant (`+`) `2.00 mm` (`68 g`) body + `12.0 Wh` `LLZO/PEO` solid-state battery** (`<15 µm` seam, `<=36.2 C` eyewear limit, `EU 2023/1542`) | Part 4, Part 17, `HARDWARE/11`, `HARDWARE/15`, `pafta/P11`, `pafta/P12` |
| **the `90°`-bent-stem B module ships seated in the box**, satisfying EU Directive (EU) 2022/2380 (`USB-C` receptacle on the delivered product) | Part 20, Section 20.3.6 |
| **the camera behind the screen is refused with numbers**: 2.9 percent transmission, a 30 percent degradation, a 4 MP class, and a panel that bends | Part 20, Section 20.4 |
| **the trial plan**, 15 tests with 30 coupons per fatigue condition, and one new fixture | Part 20, Section 20.5 |
| **the decisions R-1 to R-20**, updated for Revision L.5.0 | Part 20, Section 20.6 |
| **ten new gates**: MEC-25 to MEC-27, CAM-11 to CAM-13, DUO-7, DSP-12, DSP-13 and ENR-10, plus the two that changed subject, MEC-6 and RF-3 | Part 15, H-16, and Part 20, Section 20.7 |

**The rule of the block**. **A decision is recorded with its engineering numbers, and every drawing, 3D model, and part table agrees with it**. `[DECISION]`

### 0.78 The block of 2026-10-12: Revision L.6.0, the stretch, the `1.00 mm` bar and the projection

**The orders of Ayaz that opened Revision L.6.0**.

1. *The bar is `1 mm` in a `4 mm` module, and the body stretches with no loss of battery.*
2. *The validators run the Leaf as they run Budlum, so distance must not matter.*
3. *The camera stays `50 MP`, `508 ppi` stays, seams stay invisible, and `30 ms` holds outdoors.*
4. *The Leaf is a wallet. It connects to the nearest validator.*

| The change | Where |
|---|---|
| **the body stretches `25 %`, and `41 %` at most**: rotating PC-capped tiles keep the full `5.7 Wh`, and `7.2 Wh` with silicon anodes | Part 21, Section 21.2 |
| **`508 ppi` micro-LED tiles with lattice-continuous, invisible seams**, and Qi2.2 on the tile body | Part 21, Sections 21.2.3 to 21.2.6 |
| **the B bar is `32.0 x 4.0 x 1.00 mm` LCP-GF40 on the top edge**, in a `4.00 mm` module | Part 21, Section 21.3; `HARDWARE/05`, `HARDWARE/13` |
| **USB-C is a separately sold port part**, and the `3.5 mm` jack takes the ring rim | Part 21, Sections 21.3.4 and 21.3.6 |
| **the camera stays `50 MP`**: a small-pixel sensor in a `3.90 mm` vertical column at `f/2.0`, with focus and stabiliser | Part 21, Section 21.3.5 |
| **the strongest Wi-Fi**: stem and ring antennas as `2 x 2`, multi-link `5 + 6 GHz`, and the Leaf Dock in the room | Part 21, Section 21.4 |
| **"Zero CPU" is defined**: no general-purpose processor and no operating system; fixed-function chips and a frame store are allowed | Part 21, Section 21.5.1 |
| **the validators run the Leaf as they run the chain**: the Leaf is a wallet on the nearest validator, with zero-frame handover | Part 21, Section 21.5; research order RG-18 |
| **a full 5G standalone modem** joins the B module for `30 ms` outdoors | Part 21, Sections 21.4.2 and 21.5.7 |
| **twenty-four new gates** and four new gap items | Part 21, Section 21.8; Part 16 |

**The rule of the block**. Part 21 wins over every older part it names, until that part is rewritten. The sheets and the STL models still show L.5.0 and are listed for redraw. `[DECISION]`

**The order of Ayaz that opened Revision L.6.1, 2026-10-19**. *The main energy lives in the hardware, and the Leaf works while the hardware works.* Ayaz gave the program to the Program Lead.

| The change | Where |

| **the Host Life Law**: on a paired host with a heat path, a Leaf lives as long as the host feeds it | Part 22, Section 22.2 |
| **the Buffer tier is the first host-power tier**, and the Bare tier stays a study | Part 22, Section 22.3 |
| **the host feeds the load and the cell floats**: `7.2` times the cell life in the model | Part 22, Section 22.4 |
| **always-on needs a host that pulls heat**: the dock plate passes every case, free air passes none | Part 22, Section 22.5 |
| **the word "first" is held** until a prior art search and a patent opinion | Part 22, Section 22.11 |

**The rule of the block**. Part 22 adds to Part 21 and changes no L.6.0 number. Its findings against earlier text are in Part 22, Section 22.16. `[DECISION]`

**The orders of Ayaz that opened the L.7 candidate, 2026-10-19**. *Keep all the components of the screen in a separate hardware. The hardware can be kilometres away. Whatever is needed stays in the camera part. The battery in the Leaf is very small, and the energy is unlimited.*

# BUDLUM LEAF · THE DIRECTIVE · PART 1 · THE PRODUCT

---

## 1.1 The product law

1. A Budlum Leaf is a **surface that shows, a wallet that authorizes and a radio that connects**. `[DECISION]`
2. The body holds no processor, no operating system, no camera, no speaker, no headphone jack and no cooling part. The camera, the sound set and the jack live in the module. `[DECISION]`
3. The family shares one architecture across all sizes. A change of size is an order change, not a product change. `[DECISION]`
4. Each member name is **Budlum Leaf plus the size**. The catalogue names reference sizes, and the worn member is the Leaf Frame. `[DECISION]`
5. Device-class words (*phone*, *tablet*, *television*, *monitor*, *laptop*, *computer*, *VR headset*, *smart glasses*) never appear in claims, filenames or code. `[DECISION]`

**The acceptance test of the naming law**. A user says: "I use a Budlum Leaf." Calling the product by a device-class word is a defect of the product language.

---

## 1.2 The family

| The member | The geometry | The faces | The panel set | Density | The distance | ppd |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Leaf 80** | 80 × 80 mm | 2 | 2 × P80 | 508 ppi | 25 cm | 87 |
| **Leaf 160** | 160 × 80 mm | 2 | 2 × P160 | 508 ppi | 30 cm | 104 |
| **Leaf 160²** | 160 × 160 mm | 2 | 4 × P160 | 508 ppi | 40 cm | 130 |
| **Leaf 480** | 480 × 240 mm | 1 | 72 × T40 B | 152 ppi | 60 cm | 63 |
| **Leaf 1400** | 1 400 × 800 mm | 1 | 700 × T40 C | 61 ppi | 250 cm | 105 |
| **Leaf 2800** | 2 800 × 1 600 mm | 1 | 2 800 × T40 C | 61 ppi | 400 cm | 168 |
| **Leaf Frame** | a clear lens per eye | n.a. | 2 × a 4K class microdisplay | about 2 400 ppi | 20 mm | about 60 (Part 15, H-1) |
| The **Field** mode | a Leaf 160 or a Leaf 160² with a spacer | 2 | n.a. | n.a. | 20 mm | about 27 |

**The rule of the family**. A member is a count of panels or tiles, one bus, one or more cassettes and one hub. There is no second system. `[DECISION]`

**The size is an order parameter**. The reference sizes above are the catalogue classes, and the order fixes the number. The tooling band is a partner question, parked by the order of Ayaz. `[DECISION]`

---

## 1.3 The design language

| The property | The value | The reason |
|---|---|---|
| The corner radius (4 vertical edges) | **R = 6.35 mm** on Leaf 160 (`R 2.80 mm` on Leaf 80, `R 3.60 mm` on Leaf 240, `R 4.50 mm` on Leaf 480) | keeps a true rectangular screen (`11 947 mm²`) while removing sharp 90° corners |
| The 8 horizontal prism edges | **r = 0.80 mm radius transition** on all 4 front and 4 back perimeter edges | all 12 edges of the `4.00 mm` rectangular prism are softened for the hand |
| The upright B module edges | **r = 0.55 mm radius transition** on all 90° edges of the vertical bar and cylindrical ring | no sharp 90° edge on the `40.0 x 32.0 x 4.00 mm` top-edge module |
| The touch layer at the corner | the mesh follows the `R = 1.40 mm` screen corner with grip rejection along the `1.8 mm` bezel | a diagonal grip is not a swipe, and a corner tap still works |
| The finish | **mat polymer with an anti-fingerprint texture**; the hardness comes from the plasma coat, not from a gloss | the answer of Ayaz, question 13 |
| The colours | a deep black, a warm white, a sand, and one seasonal accent; no logo on the face | |
| The fasteners | none visible: the weld closes the body; the cassette and the module are the service paths | |
| The clear variant | the same outline in a clear or a smoky polymer with the clear panel of Part 7 | |

**The rule**. The form language is a property of the profile, so it holds at every size. `[DECISION]`

---

## 1.4 The panel law, in brief

| The panel | The geometry | The pixels | The density | The use |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **P80** | 80 × 80 mm, 0.35 mm | 1 600 × 1 600 | 508 ppi at a 50 µm pitch | Leaf 80, the Frame lens, a control surface |
| **P160** | 160 × 80 mm, 0.35 mm | 3 200 × 1 600 | 508 ppi | Leaf 160, Leaf 160² |
| **T40 B** | 40 × 40 mm, 0.35 mm | 240 × 240 | 152 ppi | Leaf 480 |
| **T40 C** | 40 × 40 mm | 96 × 96 | 61 ppi | Leaf 1400, Leaf 2800 |

**The seam law**. Tiling is for the far members, and the near members use one panel per face. A seam is invisible when the bond line is narrower than one pixel at the viewing distance. `[DECISION]` Part 2, Section 2.4.

**The refresh law**. The carried members and the Leaf 480 run **144 Hz**, variable down to 1 Hz. The Leaf 1400 and Leaf 2800 run 120 Hz and 60 Hz. A 63 in surface at 144 Hz wastes energy on changes no eye reads. `[DECISION]`

---

## 1.5 The sharpness law

```
mm per degree = d × 0.01746        ppi = 25.4 / pitch_mm        ppd = (ppi / 25.4) × mm_per_degree
```

| The member | The distance | mm per degree | ppd | The verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Leaf 80 | 250 mm | 4.36 | 87 | premium |
| Leaf 160 | 300 mm | 5.24 | 104 | premium |
| Leaf 160² | 400 mm | 6.98 | 140 | beyond the eye |
| Leaf 480 | 600 mm | 10.47 | 63 | good |
| Leaf 1400 | 2 500 mm | 43.65 | 105 | premium |
| Leaf 2800 | 4 000 mm | 69.84 | 168 | beyond the eye |

**The hybrid rule (the answer of Ayaz, question 3)**. 1. The family standard is the **angular** reading: the surface is sharp for the eye at its intended distance, and it states the number. 2. A surface watched from one metre uses a denser grade. The order states the pixels, heat, power and panel class. 3. The **linear** reading is refused with its cost written. A Leaf 1400 at 508 ppi needs 466 Mp and 330 W of addressing. That is 56 times the pixels for a difference invisible at 2.5 m. `[HYPOTHESIS]` gate DSP-2 measures the addressing curve.

---

## 1.6 The format change law

1. The identity, the protocol, the permission set and the data location never change with the format. `[DECISION]`
2. **The edge coupler** joins two surfaces: power, ground, a two lane data link, and a mechanical registration inside 0.1 mm. It is the same part on every member.
3. **The dock tree**. A Leaf 160 docks to a Leaf 480 stand. A Leaf 160² docks to a Leaf 1400 rail as a second surface.
4. **The session moves, not the device**. A session binds to the user, and a surface change has a grace period. The old surface holds the last frame until the new surface acknowledges it.
5. **The geometry manifest** states the outline, panel map, grade, seam map, keep-out band, bend band, refresh range, modes, and calibration. It is signed and stored in B.U.D. A manifest without a bend band refuses a bend claim. `[TREE]`
6. **The count rule**. Two Leaf 80 members joined at the edge behave as one Leaf 160: one session, two panels, one bus. A user buys a panel, not a product. `[DECISION]`

---

## 1.7 The product boundaries: what a Budlum Leaf is not

| The expectation | The answer | The reason |
|---|---|---|
| "it must work with no network" | it works: the safe screen, the wallet, the local echo and the optical path. Everything else is a class, and the class is stated | the network is the product's second half |
| "it must run a program" | no. The device runs signed firmware only | rule L2, and the security model |
| "it must have a camera" | it has one, in the module, and only under a lease | Part 6, Part 11 |
| "it must be waterproof" | **IP67 is the claim, and IP68 is the target**. The dual-lip FKM collar on the continuous battery sheet seals the bottom slot with or without a module fitted (Q43) | Part 3, Section 3.5, and Part 18, Section 18.8 |
| "it must be repairable by anyone" | the panel, the cassette and the module are user replaceable; the weld is a workshop operation | Part 13, Section 13.6 |
| "it must hold a week" | it holds what the configuration and the session class give, and the surface states both | Part 4, Section 4.5 |

---

## 1.8 The step-by-step of a first product definition

The procedure that turns this part into a product brief, in the order it is done. `[DECISION]`

| Step | The action | The output |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | choose the member (default: Leaf 160) | the geometry line of the brief |
| 2 | choose the cassette fill (default: Feather) | the energy line |
| 3 | choose the module state (none, camera and battery) | the mass and the runtime lines |
| 4 | copy the panel law of Section 1.4 | the panel specification request |
| 5 | copy the sharpness law of Section 1.5 | the distance and the density claim |
| 6 | copy the design language of Section 1.3 | the industrial design brief |
| 7 | copy the answer log of Part 0, Section 0.5 | the constraint list |
| 8 | write the manifest of Section 1.6 | the signed geometry record |
| 9 | attach the gate list of Part 12 | the acceptance sheet |
| 10 | hand the brief to the three lines of Part 14 | the work orders |

# BUDLUM LEAF · THE DIRECTIVE · PART 2 · THE PANEL AND THE LIGHT

---

## 2.1 The light technology

| The technology | The brightness | The lifetime | The flexibility | The class | The verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Flexible organic light emitting stack with a quantum dot colour layer** | 800 to 1 500 nits, peak on a window | 20 000 to 40 000 h | a 25 mm radius is standard in current products | 1× | **the choice** `[DECISION]` |
| Micro light emitting diode | 2 000 to 5 000 nits | above 60 000 h | good, on a stiffer backplane | 3 to 8× | the later upgrade for a bright outdoor surface |
| Transparent emissive panel | 300 to 800 nits at the eye | 10 000 to 20 000 h | on a polymer substrate | 4× | the clear family, Part 7 |
| A liquid crystal surface with a backlight | 1 000 nits | high | poor in a bend | 0.6× | refused: a Leaf bends and a backlight doubles the thickness |

`[PUBLICATION]` for the ranges, `[COMPANY]` for the supply statements, `[HYPOTHESIS]` for the Leaf values, which gates DSP-1 and DSP-5 measure.

---

## 2.2 The stack of one face, layer by layer, with the process

| Layer | The thickness | The material | The job | The process step |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Hard coat | 10 µm | a plasma deposited scratch coat | the scratch and the strain limit of the cover | a plasma deposition on the cut panel, after bonding |
| Cover | 250 µm | polycarbonate, high heat grade, optically clear | impact, optics, the outer surface | cut to the outline, printed with the R 3.20 mm corner and r 0.80 mm softened edge |
| Optical adhesive | 50 µm | a low modulus, bend rated, index matched | the neutral axis tool | a vacuum lamination at 40 °C, then a 24 h cure |
| Touch | 50 µm | metal mesh on a film | the input, with no indium tin oxide in a bend | laminated with the same adhesive |
| Emissive stack | 20 µm | a flexible organic stack with a quantum dot colour layer | the light | a printed or a deposited stack on the backplane |
| Encapsulation | 30 µm | a multilayer barrier film | below 10⁻⁶ g/m²/day of water | the thin film encapsulation, with the edge seal |
| Backplane | 30 µm | polyimide with a thin film transistor array, the row drivers, and a copper row line for 144 Hz | the address | a laser lift off from the carrier glass |
| Backing | 120 µm | polycarbonate | stiffness and the neutral axis | bonded after the encapsulation |
| **The total per face** | **0.46 mm** | | | the spine of 0.06 mm and the weld of 0.04 mm are body parts, Part 3, Section 3.2 |

`[HYPOTHESIS]` gate MEC-2 measures the real stack. **A glass part anywhere in this stack is a defect**. `[DECISION]`

---

## 2.3 How a panel is made: the process route

| Step | The operation | The parameter | The defect it prevents |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | the backplane on a carrier glass, a polyimide coat, a laser lift off | polyimide 12 to 15 µm, a lift off fluence with a stated window | a cracked backplane |
| 2 | the thin film transistor array, the row and the copper column lines | a 50 µm pitch, a 144 Hz rate, a measured line resistance | a slow charging time, a smear |
| 3 | the emissive stack and the quantum dot layer | a blue emitter with a printed red and green conversion | a narrow colour volume, a short blue life |
| 4 | the thin film encapsulation and the edge seal | below 10⁻⁶ g/m²/day, a 0.10 mm seal border | a dark spot, a growing edge failure |
| 5 | the backing lamination and the neutral axis placement | the neutral axis inside ±10 percent of the stack middle | a cracked inorganic layer in a bend |
| 6 | the cut to the outline: the R 3.20 mm corner and the 12-edge softened rectangular prism silhouette | a laser cut with a cold assist, a radius tolerance of ±0.05 mm | a chipped corner, a short crack |
| 7 | the cover lamination, the touch lamination, the vacuum step | a 50 µm adhesive, a 40 °C vacuum, a 24 h cure | a bubble, a delamination |
| 8 | the plasma hard coat | a 10 µm deposition with a measured hardness and haze | a scratch and a finger polish |
| 9 | the driver bonding and the flex attach | an anisotropic conductive film or a laser weld | an open row, a broken flex |
| 10 | the calibration and the identity write | the panel brightness, the colour, the uniformity, the bow and warp; a signed identity | a panel that cannot be compensated at the hub |
| 11 | the ageing and the test | a 24 h burn in, a photograph at three greys, a grain check | an early failure reaching the line |

**The yield rule**. A panel that fails step 10 or 11 is a coupon, not a product; it goes to the bend and the drop test pool. `[DECISION]`

---

## 2.4 The seam, and the number that decides it

| The member | One pixel at the distance | A 0.12 mm bond line | The verdict |
|---|---|---|---|
| Leaf 160 at 30 cm | 50 µm | 2.4 px | visible: one panel per face |
| Leaf 160² at 40 cm | 50 µm | 2.4 px | visible: the seam sits in the wrap zone, where the tiles already part by 1.20 mm |
| Leaf 480 at 60 cm | 166 µm | 0.72 px | invisible |
| Leaf 1400 at 2.5 m | 416 µm | 0.29 px | invisible |
| Leaf 2800 at 4 m | 416 µm | 0.29 px | invisible |

**How a far seam is made invisible**. 1. A 0.10 mm seal border instead of 0.15 mm. 2. A 0.04 mm overlap at the bond line. 3. A 30 percent wider aperture on the two edge pixel columns, so the pair reads as one wider pixel. 4. A clear, index matched, low modulus bond medium. 5. An alignment of ±20 µm in x and y and ±0.1 degree in rotation. `[HYPOTHESIS]` gate DSP-3 measures the assembled line at the intended distance.

---

## 2.5 The brightness, the colour and the refresh

| The property | The carried members | The Leaf 480 | The Leaf 1400 and 2800 |
|---|---|---|---|
| Peak brightness, a 10 percent window | 1 500 nits | 1 200 nits | 1 000 nits |
| Full field brightness | 800 nits | 800 nits | 700 nits |
| Colour volume | 92 percent of Rec.2020 | 90 percent | 85 percent |
| Refresh | 144 Hz, variable to 1 Hz | 144 Hz, variable to 1 Hz | 120 Hz, variable to 1 Hz |
| Uniformity between parts | below 2 percent | below 2 percent | below 2 percent |
| Dark line at a seam | not applicable | below the threshold | below the threshold |

**How the uniformity is achieved**. The quantum dot layer is printed per panel. Each panel carries its own calibration in its identity record, and the hub compensates at the boundary. This is the mechanism behind the 2 percent rule. `[DECISION]`

---

## 2.6 The power model, and how each term is measured

```
P_display = area × luminance × APL / (the emitter efficacy × the optical efficiency)
P_address = lines × refresh × capacitance × the panel factor
P_hub     = the decoder, the local scene renderer and the reprojection unit
P_radio   = the link class of the session
```

| The case | Display | Address | Hub | Radio | Authority | The total |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Leaf 160, a delta session at 144 Hz | 0.60 W | 0.20 W | 0.70 W | 0.40 W | 0.12 W | **2.02 W** |
| Leaf 160, a video session | 1.10 W | 0.25 W | 1.20 W | 0.60 W | 0.12 W | **3.27 W** |
| Leaf 160, a static page at 10 Hz | 0.15 W | 0.02 W | 0.10 W | 0.05 W | 0.03 W | **0.35 W** |
| Leaf 160, the safe screen, no link | 0.10 W | 0.02 W | 0.05 W | 0.00 W | 0.03 W | **0.20 W** |
| Leaf 160², a video session | 2.20 W | 0.50 W | 1.40 W | 0.60 W | 0.12 W | **4.82 W** |
| Leaf 1400, a video session at 30 percent APL | 60 W | 4 W | 6 W | 3 W | 0.5 W | **73.5 W** |

**How it is measured**. Use a calibrated luminance meter on standard images at 1, 5, 10 and 30 percent APL. Place a current probe on each rail after a 30 minute thermal soak. `[DECISION]` gate DSP-5.

---

## 2.7 The far members: how a Leaf 1400 is built

| Step | The operation | The rule |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | the tiles are bonded into columns of 20 | the bond line of Section 2.4 |
| 2 | the columns are laid on the rail | 35 rail segments, 40 cm each |
| 3 | the bus runs along the rail | the rail carries the corridor and the mount |
| 4 | the one continuous cover is laid over the face | a 0.15 mm polymer sheet, no seam visible from the front |
| 5 | the hub H3, the radio and the power input are fitted | a 4 to 9 W hub, 128 tiles maximum |
| 6 | the buffer cassette is fitted | it holds the session through a power cut |
| 7 | the calibration of every tile is read into the hub | the 2 percent uniformity rule |
| 8 | the mount plate is registered | one person, no tool, under two minutes |

**The mass of a Leaf 1400**. A 1 400 × 800 mm surface weighs **4.25 kg** (`0.9 kg` tiles, `1.4 kg` rail, `0.9 kg` cover, `0.9 kg` mount, `0.15 kg` hub). `[COMPANY]` A 65 in consumer panel surface of 2026 weighs 17 to 24 kg with its stand. Its panel rides on glass and a steel frame. `[GENERAL]`

**The heat rule**. No tile is covered by an insulating layer, and the bay has a vent path. A 73 W surface with 20 percent loss is 15 W over 11 200 cm², which is 0.0013 W/cm². Natural convection is enough.

---

## 2.8 The panel gates

| Gate | The test | The pass condition |
|---|---|---|
| DSP-1 | a P80 and a P160 panel: brightness, colour volume, lifetime estimate, bend at the class radius, 144 Hz | every value of Sections 2.5 and 2.6 |
| DSP-2 | the addressing power from 1 Hz to 144 Hz, and the copper row line | a measured curve, and the derating curve for a large surface |
| DSP-3 | the seam on a Leaf 480 and a Leaf 1400: the dark line, the luminance step, the alignment | invisible at the intended distance, alignment inside ±20 µm |
| DSP-4 | the tile replacement and the service time | under 15 minutes, and the uniformity test passes after the replacement |
| DSP-5 | the power model on a real Leaf 160, 160² and 1400 | each term measured, the total inside 10 percent |
| DSP-6 | the daylight legibility of the carried members | the stated contrast at 20 000 lux |
| DSP-7 | the variable refresh saving, 144 Hz to 1 Hz | the stated saving on a static page |
| DSP-8 | the bend durability of a Leaf 160 and a Leaf 160² | 10 000 cycles at the class radius, then 100 000, with a capacity, an image and a touch report |
| DSP-9 | the geometry manifest and the format change | a session moves between two members with no lost state and no stuck frame |
| DSP-10 | the cut quality of the R 3.20 mm corner and the r 0.80 mm softened rectangular prism edges, and the corner touch behaviour | a chip free cut, a working corner tap, and no false input from a diagonal grip |

# BUDLUM LEAF · THE DIRECTIVE · PART 3 · THE BODY

> **Revision L.6.0 note (2026-10-12)**. `FINAL/21-stretch-stem-and-projection.md` supersedes this file where they differ. The body is `128` rotating PC-capped tiles with `508 ppi` micro-LED layers and lattice-continuous seams. It stretches `25 %`, and `41 %` at most.

---

## 3.1 The mass theorem

**Every gram that is not display, not energy, not radio or not anchor is a design error**. `[DECISION]`

**The theorem of revision A, kept as the record**. `M = 9 g per 100 cm² + 2.9 g per Wh + 7.5 g`. **It is superseded by the 4-Quadrant (`+`) budget of `HARDWARE/17`**. Four `40 x 80 x 2.00 mm` (`17.0 g`) polycarbonate screen-battery quadrants (`Q1..Q4`) total **68.0 g** (`12.0 Wh` `LLZO/PEO` solid-state). The `90°`-bent-stem B module adds **20.0 g** (**88.0 g** complete). `[DECISION]`

| The configuration | The mass | The energy, delivered | Active session (`0.85 W` Zero-CPU draw) |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Single Quadrant Tile (`Q1..Q4` / Leaf 80)** (`40 x 80 x 2.00 mm`, `ISO 10993` PC + `LLZO`) | **17 g** | **`3.0 Wh` (`800 mAh`) solid-state + `Ø22 mm` Qi coil** | **3.5 h** (or eyewear module) |
| **Leaf 160 4-Quadrant (`+`) screen-battery body** (`80 x 160 x 2.00 mm`, `4 x 17 g`) | **68 g** | **`4 x 3.0 Wh = 12.0 Wh` (`3 200 mAh`) + `Ø54 mm` Qi2.2** | **>14.1 h** (portless wireless body) |
| **Leaf 160 complete (`4-Quadrant body + 90°-bent-stem B module`)** | **88 g** | **`12.0 Wh` body + `0.45 Wh` B bridge (`3 320 mAh`)** | **>14.1 h** |
| **Leaf 160 on the legacy cassette reference line** | **78 g** with the Feather cassette, **92 g** with the Standard | 16 Wh and 20 Wh | 7.9 h and 9.9 h |
| A carried surface of 2026, the same area | 190 to 230 g | 19 to 22 Wh | 6 to 9 h |

**The rule**. A mass figure belongs to a configuration, and the configuration names its energy part and its seated parts. The sentence "the device weighs X" without the sheet, the cassette and the part state is forbidden. `[DECISION]`

**The rule**. A mass figure belongs to a configuration. The sentence "the device weighs X" without the cassette and the module state is forbidden. `[DECISION]`

### 3.1.1 Where the mass went, and why the figure fell

| The part | A carried surface of 2026 | A Leaf 160 4-Quadrant (`+`) Body | The saving, in the Revision L.5.0 comparison |
|---|---|---|---|
| Cover glass 0.6 mm | 17 g | 12.8 g (`8 x 0.20 mm` `ISO 10993` PC) | shatterproof medical-grade optical polycarbonate |
| Metal frame or midframe | 20 to 30 g | 3.9 g (`(0,0)` Ti hub + `8` Ti pins + `24` N52H) | `<15 µm` (`n = 1.585`) zero-scratch `+` seam and Ti micro-dovetail locks |
| Board, processor, shields, spreader | 25 to 40 g | 3.4 g graphene/Mu-metal (`3.8 g` PCBA in B ring) | Zero CPU and Zero RAM; `U1` Master TCON + `U2` `UWB/mmWave` SiP in B ring |
| Camera island | 3 to 8 g | 0 in the body | the camera is in the flat ring of the `90°`-bent-stem B module (`20 g`) |
| Speaker, haptic parts | 3 to 6 g | 0 in the body | the sound set and sole `USB Type-C` port are in the `90°`-bent B module |
| Internal battery inside screen | 50 to 58 g | **34.4 g (`4 x 8.6 g` `LLZO/PEO` cells)** | `4 x 3.0 Wh = 12.0 Wh` non-flammable solid-state cells (`EU 2023/1542` `<3 s` swap) |
| T-OLED panels, PDLC, AgNW/ITO, Qi2.2 coils | 14 to 20 g | 17.5 g | dual T-OLED (`6.8 g`), OCA (`1.6 g`), PDLC (`1.9 g`), AgNW (`1.6 g`), `Qi2.2` (`5.6 g`) |
| **The total** | **190 to 230 g** | **68 g** 4-Quadrant body (**88 g** complete) | the reading of the detailed pass of `HARDWARE/17` |

---

## 3.2 The body stack, from the outside in

```
face A: coat 0.01 + cover 0.15 + adhesive 0.05 + touch 0.05 + panel 0.10 + backing 0.10 = 0.46 mm
core:   the cassette 2.88 mm, in the bay
face B: the same 0.46 mm
spine:  0.06 mm shared at the edges and the wrap zone
seal and weld: 0.09 mm at the perimeter
        -----------------------------------------------
        the closed body: 4.00 mm  (the answer of Ayaz, K1)
```

| The part | The value |
|---|---|
| The leaf alone | 4.00 mm |
| The cassette | 2.88 mm, fixed |
| The module | 4.00 mm |
| The attached stack | 8.00 mm, and every claim names the part |
| The visible edge | a 0.9 mm bead at the weld |
| The bend | the wrap circle is **R 25.5 mm** and the gentle bend is **R 70 mm** and free. There is no hinge and no crease line |

---

## 3.3 The spine: one laminate, five jobs

| Layer | The thickness | The material | The job |
|---|---|---|---|
| Carrier | 60 µm | 301 stainless steel, perforated to 40 percent open area inside the wrap zone | stiffness where it is flat, no stiffness in the wrap |
| Conductor | 18 µm | copper | power and the video lane reference |
| Dielectric | 12 µm | a bend rated polyimide | insulation |
| The antenna windows | openings | through the laminate, one per antenna | the radio passes the metal |
| The anchor pattern | a 0.4 mm pole pitch | in the steel | the module and the mount hold |

**How it is made**. 1. The steel is etched with the perforation and the anchor pattern in one pass. 2. The copper is laminated and etched to the bus pattern. 3. The polyimide is bonded. 4. The laminate is cut to the edge profile with the R 3.20 mm corner. 5. The windows are punched. 6. The wrap zone is formed to R 25.5 mm and relaxed. 7. The continuity and the resistance of every lane are tested. The mass is 0.85 g per 100 cm², so 1.1 g in a Leaf 160. `[HYPOTHESIS]` gate MEC-5.

---

## 3.4 The bend, and the slip rule

1. The layers are **not bonded into one beam** across their whole area. Each layer bends about its **own** thin axis, and the interfaces shear by design.
2. **The numbers**. No interface sees more than **0.4 to 0.8 MPa of shear**. Each interface carries **0.35 to 0.6 mm of slip at R 25.5 mm**. `[HYPOTHESIS]` gates B-5 and B-6 measure both.
3. **Why it is required**. A bonded 4.00 mm stack at R 25.5 mm puts **7.8 percent** on its outer fibre. Nothing in this program survives that. Decoupled, the worst layer is the panel tile at **0.63 percent**.
4. **How the slip is built**. The layers register at the ends, and each interface carries a low-modulus silicone slip film. The 4 continuous polymer-gel battery layers slide over 3 `50 µm` silicone interlayers.
5. **The failure modes to watch**. Watch creep at 60 °C over 1 000 hours and film wicking into the display gap. Also watch foreign particles and interlayer wear over 10 000 wraps. Each has a test in gates B-5, B-6 and B-1.

---

## 3.5 The edge, the seal and the impact

| The property | The value | The how |
|---|---|---|
| The edge | an ultrasonic weld of the two polymer covers at a 1.5 mm land, a 0.4 mm bead | a horn at 20 kHz, a pressure and a hold time with a stated window, and a pull test on a sample from every shift |
| The class | **IP67 is the claim, and IP68 is the target**, measured with a module fitted and with none | a two bead gasket on the energy sheet carrier, a sealed connector, a labyrinth at the module contacts and the vent membrane of gate MEC-24 |
| The old refusal, withdrawn | the earlier revision refused IP68, because a bay with a lid and a water tight body are contradictory. **The energy sheet has no bay and no lid: the carrier is the seal**. The refusal is therefore withdrawn (the audit F-08) | route A+ of Part 17, Section 17.6, and gate MEC-19 |
| The impact | 78 g at 1.2 m is 0.9 J | the cover is the impact layer, the spine carries the load to the weld, the cassette floats on a 0.2 mm elastic land |
| The drop set | 6 faces, 1.2 m, concrete, with a cassette and with a module | gate MEC-4, with a damage report per face |

---

## 3.6 The cassette bay, mechanically

| The element | The specification |
|---|---|
| The bay | a 2.88 mm slot inside the outline, with a 0.2 mm elastic land and a lead-in chamfer |
| The slide | one straight motion at the fixed edge, 18 mm of travel, no tool, target below 15 s |
| The contacts | 8 spring contacts, wiped and released on the slide, plus 2 sense lines, gold on gold |
| The latch | a polymer snap with a magnetic assist and a secondary mechanical catch, rated 1 000 cycles |
| The gasket | a 0.5 mm lip seal, replaced with the cassette service interval |
| The detection | the device reads the cassette identity at the first contact and *before* it enables the power path |

**The rule of the first contact**. A cassette is identified before it is used, and a cassette whose record fails is refused with the failing field on the surface. `[DECISION]` Part 4, Section 4.4.

---

## 3.7 How a Leaf 160 is assembled: the line procedure

| Step | The operation | The parameter | The check |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | the panel pair arrives calibrated with its identity | the calibration hash | the identity read |
| 2 | the spine is bonded to face A and face B at the edge zones | a 20 s press at 80 °C | the pull test on a sample |
| 3 | the bus is connected and the hub is placed in the keep-out band | a 1 mm alignment window | the continuity and the enumeration |
| 4 | the two faces are brought together with the cassette bay former in place | the bay former is a 2.88 mm gauge | the slot gauge per unit |
| 5 | the ultrasonic weld closes the perimeter | the horn window, a bead of 0.4 mm | a bead photograph and a leak test on a sample |
| 6 | the cassette is fitted and identified | the record read and verified | the power path enabled |
| 7 | the module is fitted, if ordered | the anchor force and the contact wipe | the module record and the antenna check |
| 8 | the full function test and the calibration | the screen, the touch, the radio, the radio frequency power, the bend | the test record |
| 9 | the ageing: a 2 h burn in at 45 °C with a moving image | the temperature and the current | the defect screen |
| 10 | the packing and the record upload to B.U.D. | the serial, the cassette, the module, the panel identities | the manifest signed |

**The yield rule**. A unit that fails step 8 twice goes to the teardown pool, and the failure is written into the record. `[DECISION]`

---

## 3.8 The user service procedures, written for a person

| The task | The tool | The time | The rule |
|---|---|---|---|
| Replace the cassette | none | below 15 s | the device is powered down by the latch event, or stays alive on the buffer capacitor for a stated time |
| Replace the module | none | below 10 s | the session keeps the state on the validator |
| Replace the panel | the kit tool and the guide | 20 to 30 min | the panel is a field replaceable unit; the corner alignment uses the two registration pins |
| Replace the battery cells | no; the cassette is the unit | n.a. | a cell swap inside a cassette is a workshop operation, and the gauge record makes an unauthorized swap visible |
| Replace the pen | none | n.a. | the pen pairs again through the module |

**The five year rule**. Spare parts are held for at least five years after the last sale of a member, and the parts list is public. `[DECISION]` (the answer of Ayaz, question 34 and the European removability rule.)

---

## 3.9 The body gates

| Gate | The test | The pass condition |
|---|---|---|
| MEC-1 | the bend durability: a bonded stack against a decoupled stack, at R 25.5 mm and at R 70 mm | the stated cycles, the measured pair, and no interface wicking. B-1 and B-2 carry the product claim |
| MEC-2 | the mass of a real Leaf 160, part by part | inside 10 percent of the law of Section 3.1 |
| MEC-3 | the cassette: the removal time, 1 000 cycles, the contact resistance, the record | below 15 s, no damage, the record complete |
| MEC-4 | the drop set | a working surface, with a damage report |
| MEC-5 | the spine: stiffness, heat spread, the antenna windows, the anchor force | every value stated |
| MEC-6 | the charge heat and the power ceiling at 10, 25, 45, 60 and 80 W, seated and removed | the derating curve, a surface inside the safety limit |
| MEC-7 | the seal class and the dust behaviour: **IP67 claimed, IP68 as the target**, with a module fitted and with none | the class claimed is the class measured, and gate MEC-19 owns the number |
| MEC-8 | the radio set: the free hold, the hand hold, the desense, the module gain, the match flat and bent | every budget of Part 5, Section 5.3 |
| MEC-9 | the energy accounting on a real device | each term measured, the total inside 10 percent |
| MEC-10 | the pen: the attach, the charge, the latency, the pressure, the magnet effect on the antenna | the numbers of Part 6, Section 6.6, and no radio penalty |
| MEC-11 | the module thermal path: the spreader, the interface pad, the latent buffer at 35 °C ambient | the module rise, the leaf rise and the cell temperature inside their limits |
| MEC-12 | the capture policy: a hot start, a long take, a cooldown, the message | the refusal works and the user sees the reason |
| MEC-13 | the soft corner: a diagonal grip on 20 users, in a pocket, a hand and a bag | no accidental input, and no corner tap missed |

---

# BUDLUM LEAF · THE DIRECTIVE · PART 4 · THE 4-QUADRANT (+) BIOCOMPATIBLE SOLID-STATE BATTERY AND QI2.2 WIRELESS CHARGING

> **Revision L.6.0 note (2026-10-12)**. `FINAL/21-stretch-stem-and-projection.md` supersedes this file where they differ. The body holds `5.7` to `7.2 Wh` in `128` micro cells, with no loss to the stretch. The B module adds `0.14 Wh` and the dock carries a `20 Wh` pack.

---

## 4.1 The mechanical, electrical, and eyewear-safety standard (`HARDWARE/11`, `HARDWARE/15`)

| The property | The value |
|---|---|
| The dimensions & mass | `4 x (40.0 x 80.0 x 2.00 mm)` snap-together polycarbonate quadrants (`17.0 g` each) = **`80.0 x 160.0 x 2.00 mm` (`68.0 g` total body)** |
| The cell chemistry | **non-flammable `LLZO/PEO-Ceramic` all-solid-state cells** (`4 x 3.0 Wh = 12.0 Wh` / `3 200 mAh` at `750 Wh/L`, `0%` liquid, `0.0 ppm` HF gas) |
| Eyewear & ocular safety | **`<=36.2 °C` hardware NTC cut-off**, **`ISO 10993-5/-10`** medical-grade BPA-free PC, `<0.08 µT` bifilar ELF, **`SAR = 0.054 W/kg`**, `IEC 62471` RG0 |
| The `+` cross optical seam | **`12 µm` (`<15 µm`) phenyl-fluorosilicone self-healing lip (`n = 1.585` vs `n_PC = 1.586`, Fresnel `R < 0.0001%`)**, `<2.5 µm` Z-step (zero scratch/line) |
| Tool-free quadrant hot-swap | **`(0,0)` Ti hub + `8` Ti pins + `24` N52H (`11.2 N`) + `64` gold leaves** (`<3 s` swap, `EU 2023/1542` Art. 11) |
| Universal wireless charging | **Central `Ø54.0 mm` 4-sector `Qi2.2` MPP (`15-25 W`)** + **`4 x Ø22.0 mm` (`5-10 W`) local `Qi` coils**; **zero USB-C and zero buttons on body** |
| Sole `USB-C` & Zero-CPU core | **sole `USB Type-C` (`40 W`)** in the `90°` B stem; `U1` TCON + `U2` `UWB / mmWave` SiP in the B ring (`20 g`) |
| Screen bending strain | **`0.38 percent` outer polycarbonate strain (`0.22%` `LLZO` strain) at `R 25.5 mm`** via `G = 0.08 MPa` silicone OCA slip layers |

---

## 4.2 The modular 4-Quadrant (`+`) solid-state battery family

| The member | `+` Screen-battery body | Solid-state cell density | Target capacity | Delivered energy | Body mass | Active session (`0.85 W`) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Leaf 80 (1 tile)** | `40 x 80 x 2.00 mm` (`17 g`) | `750 Wh/L` `LLZO/PEO` solid-state | `800 mAh` | **3.0 Wh** | `17 g` | **3.5 h** (or eyewear visor) |
| **Leaf 160 (4 tiles)** | `80 x 160 x 2.00 mm` (`68 g`) | `750 Wh/L` `LLZO/PEO` solid-state | **`3 200 mAh`** | **12.0 Wh** | `68 g` | **>14.1 h** |
| **Leaf 160² (8 tiles)** | `160 x 160 x 2.00 mm` (`144 g`) | `750 Wh/L` `LLZO/PEO` solid-state | `6 400 mAh` | **24.0 Wh** | `144 g` | **>22.0 h** |
| **Leaf 480 (48 tiles)** | `320 x 480 x 2.40 mm` | `750 Wh/L` `LLZO/PEO` solid-state | `38 400 mAh` | **144.0 Wh** | modular | **>30.0 h** |

`[DECISION]` Cancelling the bulky bottom pack and splitting the `2.00 mm` (`68 g`) body across `+` creates four `17 g` tiles (`Q1..Q4`). Gates ENR-1 and ENR-2 measure the `12.0 Wh` `LLZO/PEO` capacity, `Qi2.2` wireless charging, and `<3 s` quadrant swap.

| The architecture class | The density / bend behavior | The source standing |
|---|---|---|
| Non-flammable `LLZO/PEO` all-solid-state cell inside each polycarbonate tile | **`750 Wh/L`**, delivering **`3.0 Wh` (`800 mAh`) per tile = `12.0 Wh` (`3 200 mAh`) total** (`0%` liquid, `0` HF) | `[DECISION]` |
| `2.00 mm` (`68.0 g`) 11-layer `ISO 10993` polycarbonate + `T-OLED` + `PDLC` stack | **`0.38%` outer polycarbonate strain (`0.22%` `LLZO` strain) at `R 25.5 mm`** via `G = 0.08 MPa` OCA slip | `[DECISION]` |
| `<15 µm` (`n = 1.585`) `+` cross optical seam + `(0,0)` Ti hub + `64` gold leaves | Fresnel `R < 0.0001%` (zero visible line/scratch), `<2.5 µm` Z-step, **`<3 s` tool-free swap (`EU 2023/1542` Art. 11)** | `[DECISION]` |
| Bulky external bottom battery module (`80 x 36 x 4 mm`) | **cancelled in Revision L.5.0**: replaced by 4-Quadrant (`+`) internal solid-state cells + `Qi2.2` wireless body | `[DECISION]` |

---

## 4.3 Why the 4-Quadrant (`+`) solid-state architecture solves ergonomics, eyewear safety, and EU replaceability

1. Cancelling the bulky bottom pack restores a clean **`2.00 mm` (`68 g`)** outline (`4 x 17 g` tiles). `[DECISION]`
2. Each tile combines **`ISO 10993` polycarbonate**, **flicker-free `T-OLED`**, **`PDLC` privacy film**, **`AgNW/ITO` mesh**, and a **`3.0 Wh` `LLZO/PEO` solid-state cell**.
3. The `+` seam uses a **`12 µm` (`n = 1.585`) self-healing lip** and **Corning edge-wrap traces** (`0.00 mm` internal bezel).
4. Eyewear safety uses **`0%` flammable liquid**, **`<=36.2 °C` NTC cut-off**, **`<0.08 µT` bifilar tabs**, and **`SAR = 0.054 W/kg`**.
5. The portless body charges on any **`Qi / Qi2.2` (`15-25 W`)** pad. The **sole `USB Type-C` (`40 W`)** lives in the `90°`-bent B stem. `[DECISION]` gate ENR-1.

---

## 4.4 The gauge and the record

| The field | The source | Why it matters |
|---|---|---|
| The part class and the part id | the secure element | an anchor and a revocation |
| The cell chemistry and the batch | the maker | a recall can be scoped |
| The rated capacity and the measured capacity | the maker and the gauge | a mismatch beyond the tolerance refuses the cassette |
| The cycle count and the depth of discharge history | the gauge | a worn cassette is visible before it fails |
| The temperature history, the peak and the time above 45 °C | the gauge | the heat record |
| The protection events: over current, over voltage, over temperature | the protection circuit | a hard failure leaves a trace |
| The signatures: the maker, the line, the calibration | the maker and the device | a counterfeit shell is detected |
| The reference: the pair of the cassette with the leaf serial | the device | a stolen cassette is visible |

**How the gauge is protected**. The gauge is sealed inside the cassette shell, its key is burned at the line, and it signs every reading with a monotonic counter. A rewritten record breaks the chain, and the device refuses the cassette. `[DECISION]` gate ENR-3.

---

## 4.5 The charging ladder

| The path | The input | The heat at 80 percent efficiency | The note |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Wired, a USB-C receptacle at the fixed edge** | **40 W peak, 25 W flat**, with a 45 W ceiling in the hand | about 0.5 W at 25 W, and 9 to 13 W at the 45 W ceiling | the fast path, the service path and the common charger rule of the European Union |
| **The dock and the rail, by contact** | **25 W**, 20 V at 1.25 A per power pad | **about 0.1 W** at 30 mΩ, and 0.31 W at a worn 100 mΩ pad | four gold pads on the fixed edge, and spring pins in the dock. **This path replaces the coil** |
| the wireless paths | **withdrawn** | n.a. | **the order of Ayaz of 2026-10-06: no wireless charging**. Part 20, Section 20.1, states what it cost and what the withdrawal returns |

**The derating curve**. Full power holds below 38 °C of the surface. Power derates linearly to 20 percent at 45 °C and stops above 48 °C with a message. `[DECISION]` gate MEC-6 fixes the numbers.

**Why the heat is the ceiling and not the connector**. Three thermal paths are measured: through the leaf, through the battery back, and along the sheet to the edge. At 80 W and 80 percent, 16 W over 200 cm² is 0.08 W/cm², which needs the spreader of Part 11.

---

## 4.6 The energy accounting, per member

| The member and the case | Display | Address | Hub | Radio | Authority | The total | The cassette | The runtime |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Leaf 80, a delta session | 0.35 W | 0.10 W | 0.60 W | 0.35 W | 0.10 W | 1.50 W | Feather 20 Wh | 10.7 h |
| Leaf 160, a delta session | 0.60 W | 0.20 W | 0.70 W | 0.40 W | 0.12 W | 2.02 W | Feather 20 Wh | 7.9 h |
| Leaf 160, a delta session, with the module | the same | | | | | 2.02 W | 40 Wh | 15.8 h |
| Leaf 160, a video session | 1.10 W | 0.25 W | 1.20 W | 0.60 W | 0.12 W | 3.27 W | Standard 25 Wh | 6.1 h |
| Leaf 160, a static page at 10 Hz | 0.15 W | 0.02 W | 0.10 W | 0.05 W | 0.03 W | 0.35 W | Feather 20 Wh | 45 h |
| Leaf 160, the safe screen, no link | 0.10 W | 0.02 W | 0.05 W | 0.00 W | 0.03 W | 0.20 W | Feather 20 Wh | 80 h |
| Leaf 160², a video session | 2.20 W | 0.50 W | 1.40 W | 0.60 W | 0.12 W | 4.82 W | 2 × Feather | 13.3 h |
| Leaf 480, a static work session | 5.00 W | 0.80 W | 1.60 W | 0.60 W | 0.12 W | 8.12 W | the rail | mains |
| Leaf 1400, a video session | 60 W | 4 W | 6 W | 3 W | 0.50 W | 73.5 W | the rail | mains |

**The rule**. A runtime claim names the member, the cassette, the session class and the term table. The phrase "all day" is forbidden. `[DECISION]`

---

## 4.7 The safety and the rules of the market

| The rule | The subject | The design answer |
|---|---|---|
| IEC 62133-2 | the cells and the cassette | the abuse set of gate ENR-4: over charge, over discharge, a short, a crush, a nail, a thermal chamber |
| UN 38.3 | the transport of the cells and the cassette | every shipment, and a test summary in the record |
| IEC 62368-1 | the electrical safety of the product | the double insulation at the connector and the fault test |
| EU 2023/1542, Article 11, from 18 February 2027 | a removable and a replaceable battery | **the slide out cassette is the design answer**, and the answer is written into the claim file |
| The common charger rule | the wired path | USB-C at the fixed edge |
| RoHS and REACH | the materials | with the panel and the cell partners |
| The battery passport | portables are outside its scope today | it is watched, and the record format already carries the fields |

---

## 4.8 How the 4-Quadrant (`+`) solid-state screen-battery tiles are built

| Step | The operation | The parameter |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | `35.0 x 75.0 x 1.08 mm` non-flammable `LLZO/PEO` solid-state cells (`3.0 Wh` each) are hermetically sealed with bifilar tabs | **`4 x 3.0 Wh = 12.0 Wh` (`3 200 mAh`)**, `0%` liquid, `0.0 ppm` HF gas |
| 2 | `Qi2.2` `90°` sector coil (`Ø54 mm`), local `Ø22 mm` `Qi` coil, `U_QI` BMS, `36.2 °C` NTCs, and `Mu-metal`/graphene shield are laminated | `<0.08 µT` stray ELF field and `15-25 W` `Qi2.2` wireless charging |
| 3 | front/back `T-OLED` (`0.14 mm`), `AgNW/ITO` (`0.04 mm`), `PDLC` (`0.09 mm`), and `ISO 10993` PC (`0.20 mm`) are bonded with `G = 0.08 MPa` OCA | `2.20 ± 0.02 mm` tile stack (`17.0 g` per tile), `0.38%` strain at `R 25.5 mm` |
| 4 | Corning edge-wrapped conductors are formed on the inner `+` faces and coated with the `12 µm` (`n = 1.585`) phenyl-fluorosilicone optical lip | Fresnel reflection `R < 0.0001%`, zero internal bezel, zero scratch |
| 5 | `(0,0)` Ti hub seat, tapered Ti pins, `6` bipolar `N52H` magnets, and `16` gold micro-leaf contacts are fitted on each internal edge | `<2.5 µm` Z-step, `11.2 N` pull hold, `3 ms` Dead-Pad, `IP68` seal |
| 6 | formation cycling, `3 ms` Dead-Pad authentication burn-in, `<=36.2 °C` ocular thermal lock test, and `<3 s` hot-swap verification | `12.0 Wh` capacity and serial recorded into the cryptographic element |
| 7 | batch verification on the ENR-4 abuse set, `IP68` immersion, and `10 000`-bend 4-quadrant flex test | passes `IP68` (`1.5 m`, `30 min`) and `0.38%` strain at `R 25.5 mm` |

---

## 4.9 The energy gates

| Gate | The test | The pass condition |
|---|---|---|
| ENR-1 | the `4 x 3.0 Wh = 12.0 Wh` (`3 200 mAh`, `750 Wh/L`) `LLZO/PEO` solid-state battery capacity and impedance | **`>=12.0 Wh` (`3 200 mAh`)** at Leaf 160 (`68.0 g` 4-tile body), resistance `<28 mΩ` |
| ENR-2 | the `2.00 mm` (`68 g`) 4-Quadrant (`+`) flex at **`R 25.5 mm` over `10 000` bends** and **`<3 s` tool-free swap** | **`0.38 percent` max PC strain (`0.22%` `LLZO` strain)**, zero reboot during `<3 s` swap |
| ENR-3 | the gauge and `3 ms` Dead-Pad cryptographic handshake against a counterfeit shell | pins remain at `0 V` until `<3 ms` handshake passes; refuses mismatched record |
| ENR-4 | the abuse set: over charge, over discharge, a short, a crush, a nail, a thermal chamber | no fire, `0.0 ppm` HF gas, `<=36.2 °C` eyewear limit, and a report per case |
| ENR-5 | the `10 000` cycle `+` seam magnetic latch (`11.2 N`), `64` gold leaves, and `<15 µm` (`n = 1.585`) `IP68` lip | contact resistance `<18 mΩ`, step `<2.5 µm`, passes `IP68` after `10 000` swaps |
| ENR-6 | the charge from 0 to 80 percent via `Qi2.2` wireless (`15-25 W`), `90°` B stem USB-C (`40 W`), and Dock (`27 W`) | follows the derating curve with eyewear temperature `<=36.2 °C` |
